2	
3	Группа = **бренд + тип вещи + модель**. Тип входит в ключ группы, поэтому шапка и
4	подвеска с одинаковым словом «Dagger» никогда не попадают в одну группу. Модели
5	бывают двух уровней: линейка («Balenciaga Track») и версии внутри неё (Track 2,
6	Track Runner). Результат — чистая функция от объявления и правил: полная
7	перегруппировка 100 тыс. объявлений занимает около 30 секунд, повторный проход без
8	изменений меняет 0 строк.

569	def tail_sample(lines: Sequence[LineStat], *, after: int, size: int, seed: int) -> list[LineStat]:
570	    """Lines past ``after`` drawn with replacement in proportion to sales.
571	
572	    The share of garbage among the draws estimates the garbage share of tail sales.
573	    """
574	
575	    tail = [item for item in lines[after:] if item.sales > 0]
576	    if not tail:
577	        return []
578	    return random.Random(seed).choices(tail, weights=[item.sales for item in tail], k=size)

  └─ [РЕЗУЛЬТАТ Read]: 1	# Seed model dictionaries
2	
3	One file per brand, named by the brand slug. Seeds are imported as confirmed groups when a
4	brand is grouped; existing groups (including ones the user merged or marked "not a model")
5	are never overwritten or recreated.
6	
7	```yaml
8	models:
9	  - name: Geobasket            # display name, also an alias
10	    aliases: [geo basket]      # extra spellings; normalized like titles
11	    types: [hitop_sneakers]    # taxonomy types, or "any" for every type of the brand
12	    infer: hitop_sneakers      # optional: type to pick when a category is ambiguous
13	    parent: Track              # optional: the line this model is a version of
14	```
15	
16	These seeds were written from known product names, not from mined live data. After the
17	first live collection, review `python -m app.cli grouping-report --brand <name>` and the
18	"auto" groups in the UI, then add confirmed names here.

173	    """Uniform, stable sampling order, independent of the dev/holdout split."""
174	
175	    return hashlib.sha256(f"sample:{grailed_id}".encode()).hexdigest()
176

178	def name_keys(text: str) -> frozenset[str]:
179	    """Spelling-insensitive keys: "Speed Hunters", "Speedhunters" and "speed-hunters" match."""
180	
181	    tokens = _TOKEN.findall(fold(text))
182	    keys = {"".join(tokens), "".join(singular(token) for token in tokens)}
183	    return frozenset(key for key in keys if key)

870	    def _wholesale_sellers(self, rows: Sequence[_Row]) -> set[str]:
871	        """Sellers whose volume (policy ``wholesale_listings``) keeps them out of mining."""
872	
873	        limit = self._policy.mining.wholesale_listings
874	        if limit is None:
875	            return set()
876	        counts = Counter(row.seller for row in rows if row.seller)

[2026-10-07T23:09:42.925Z] [ФОНОВАЯ ЗАДАЧА ЗАВЕРШЕНА]: Agent "Review grouping-v7 step 2 diff" finished
--- Результат фоновой задачи ---
I found one real defect (MEDIUM) and two latent LOW issues. I found no invariant breaks in the descriptor flow. I read the full diff and the four new files. I also checked three things against the shipped config:
- All 227 descriptor phrases (215 global, 12 for Balenciaga) round-trip through `TitleNormalizer.phrase`.
- No phrase is claimed by two descriptors in the same product type.
- The `article_code` filter hits no seed name or alias.

**[MEDIUM] Split with `as_version` can make a description group the parent of a user model**
File: `backend/app/api/groups.py:274`
```python
source = await _group(session, group_id)   # line 253, not _editable
...
if payload.as_version and source.slug != NONE_SLUG:
    parent_id = source.parent_id or source.id
```
- `_editable` now rejects `_desc-*` groups, but `split_group` still loads its source with `_group`, so splitting a description group is allowed. That seems intended: it lets a user promote "Wide Leg" to a model.
- The `as_version` guard only excludes `_none`. Splitting `_desc-wide-leg` with `as_version=true` creates a confirmed user group whose `parent_id` is the description group.
- `_check_parent` blocks exactly this through `_editable` on the manual set-parent path. Here a version ends up under a group that `_dictionary` ignores, that `trends` hides, and that is retired when unused. The version then sits under a hidden or retired parent.
- Fix: use `source.slug != NONE_SLUG and not is_descriptor_slug(source.slug)`. Alternatively, return 422 `as_version` for service groups.

**[LOW] Brand marks and global descriptors are not checked against each other**
Files: `backend/app/services/grouping/policy.py:134-146` and `backend/app/services/grouping/descriptors.py:152`
- `_check_unique` runs separately per call: once for each brand's `generic:` list and once for the global list.
- `GroupingPolicy.descriptor_index` then concatenates the two lists.
- If a brand mark repeats a global phrase for the same type, `PhraseIndex.add` silently keeps the first entry (the global one). The brand's class and name never apply, and no error is raised.
- If the two entries have different names but the same slug, you get one group with a surprising name.
- It is latent today. A script confirmed there are no overlaps for Balenciaga.
- Fix: run the uniqueness check over the combined tuple when building the policy. A single-line check per brand is enough.

**[LOW] `mining.wholesale_listings` does nothing under the `none` seller privacy mode**
File: `backend/app/services/grouping/service.py:727-734`
- `Counter(row.seller ... if row.seller)` is empty when `store_seller_identity == "none"`, so the filter is silently inactive.
- The existing mining path falls back to `listing:{id}` per listing in the same situation.
- Fix: add one sentence to `docs/GROUPING.md` and the YAML comment saying the filter needs seller identity (hashed or plain). The setting is off by default.

**Checks that came out clean**
- **Convergence:**
  - Descriptor rows are retried through `_NO_MODEL` and re-described after every mining step.
  - `_described` groups retire and revive through the same `used` and `was_retired` path as mined groups, and the rules hash includes `retired_at`.
  - In a full pass nothing is carried over from a previous pass, so the second pass sees the same input.
  - In a delta pass, stored `descriptor` rows are not targets, and `_describe` only touches rows whose method is `none`.
- **Tombstones:** the `ignored` check in `_describe` is unreachable through the API, since `_editable` returns 409 and `not-model` and `merge` both go through it. It is harmless.
- **SQL:** the trends filter uses `startswith(..., autoescape=True)`, so the `_` in `_desc-` is escaped and the query is not built from strings. The `iif` in `reports.py` is SQLite-specific, which matches the project.
- **PII:** the reports read `seller_identity` into memory only and never print it. The CLI output contains phrases and counts.
- **Split and regroup parity:** `load_brand_terms` produces the same term set for the split API as the `_rows` designers do for regroup, so the two normalizers agree.
- **Mining:**
  - `_forbidden_mask` is O(tokens × distinct phrase lengths), which is fine.
  - Ranking existing groups first in `ranked` is the right hysteresis fix.
  - Masking user-blocked `ModelBlock` phrases (they are passed as `generic_phrases`) is a deliberate behaviour change, consistent with the new descriptor handling.

**Эксперимент с оптовиками**

В данных Balenciaga 17 продавцов выставляют по 500 и больше объявлений. Это 76% всех объявлений, но только 8% продаж. Они могут засорять майнинг: когда у одного оптовика сотня одинаковых объявлений, фраза из них может набрать нужное число «продавцов» и стать линейкой. Проверка отвечает на вопрос, станет ли чище, если исключить этих продавцов из майнинга.

Что произойдёт при запуске:
1. Я включаю `wholesale_listings: 500` в `config/grouping.yaml` и делаю копию базы `data/cache/eval/s2_w.db`. Рабочая `data/grailed.db` не затрагивается.
2. Прогоняю два полных прохода по копии, потом оценку на dev (около 3 минут).
3. Сравниваю результат с текущим: точность модели 64,9%, мусор в топ-50 8% / 3,02%, полнота линий 81,4%, потерянные продажи 0.

Правило решения записано в плане заранее: оптовиков исключаем, только если мусора становится меньше, а полнота линий и потерянные продажи остаются в целях. В прошлом прототипе число auto-групп упало с 3 066 до 1 347, но ещё 661 продажа ушла в «Без модели» (Stomper, Technoclog, Le Cagole belt). Эксперимент покажет, окупается ли такая потеря. Холдаут не трогаю, только dev.

Запустить? Если да, результат запишу в план, а строку в конфиге оставлю или уберу по правилу решения.
